$(function() {
$('#page').on('click', '.acknowledgeAnnouncement', function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    var announcement_id = $(this).closest('div.alert').data('announcement-id');

    Cookies.set('announcement_' + announcement_id, 'closed', { expires: 1 });
});

$.each(Cookies.get(), function (cookie_name, cookie_value) {
    if (cookie_name.indexOf('announcement_') >= 0) {
        if (cookie_value == "closed") {
            announcement_id = cookie_name.split('_')[1];
            $('div.alert[data-announcement-id="' + announcement_id + '"]').remove();
        }
    }
});

$(document).on('ready', function (e) {
    function getValueName(slider_id, weight) {
        found = "ERROR";
        $('#' + slider_id + '_rslider_vals').find('span').each(function (e, i) {
            if ($(this).data('weight') == weight) {
                found = $(this).data('valname');
                return false;
            }
        });

        return found;
    }

    function getValueNames(slider_id, selection) {
        ret = [];

        for (i = 0; i < selection.length; i++) {
            ret.push(getValueName(slider_id, parseInt(selection[i])));
        }

        return ret;
    }

    function getValueID(slider_id, weight) {
        found = "ERROR";
        $('#' + slider_id + '_rslider_vals').find('span').each(function (e, i) {
            if ($(this).data('weight') == weight) {
                found = $(this).data('valid');
                return false;
            }
        });

        return found;
    }

    range_sliders_attr_ids = []
    $('.rangeslider').each(function (i, e) {
        var current_slider_id = $(this).prop('id');
        var current_slider = document.getElementById(current_slider_id + '_rslider');

        try {
            noUiSlider.create(current_slider, {
                start: 0,
                step: 1,
                connect: true,
                // tooltips: true,
                range: {
                    'min': $('#' + current_slider_id + '_rslider_min_max').data('min'),
                    'max': $('#' + current_slider_id + '_rslider_min_max').data('max')
                },
                start: [$('#' + current_slider_id + '_rslider_min_max').data('handle1'), $('#' + current_slider_id + '_rslider_min_max').data('handle2')]
            }, true);

            // events
            current_slider.noUiSlider.on('update', function (values, handle) {
                var selection = current_slider.noUiSlider.get();

                valueNames = getValueNames(current_slider_id, selection);

                start = valueNames[0];
                finish = valueNames[1];

                text_output = "<b>" + start + "</b> <i class=\"fa fa-arrows-h\"></i> <b>" + finish + "</b>";

                $('#' + current_slider_id + '_rslider_text').html(text_output);
            });

            current_slider.noUiSlider.on('change', function (values, handle) { // finalizare - trimitem pe form pretty much <3
                var attr_id = current_slider_id;

                var selection = current_slider.noUiSlider.get();
                lowerBoundary = parseInt(selection[0]);
                highBoundary = parseInt(selection[1]);

                $('input[name="' + attr_id + '[]"]:checkbox').removeAttr('checked');

                attr_vals = [];
                for (i = lowerBoundary; i <= highBoundary; i++) {
                    var val_id = getValueID(current_slider_id, i);
                    if (val_id != "ERROR") {
                        attr_vals.push(val_id);

                        $('input[name="' + attr_id + '[]"]').filter('[value=' + val_id + ']').prop('checked', true);
                    }
                }

                $('form[id=applyCategoryFilters]').submit();
            });
        } catch (err) {
        }
    });
});


//open/close lateral filter
$('#cd-filter-trigger').on('click', function () {
    triggerFilter(true);
    $([document.documentElement, document.body]).animate({
        scrollTop: $("#applyCategoryFilters").offset().top
    }, 700);
});
$('#cd-filter .cd-close').on('click', function () {
    triggerFilter(false);
});

function triggerFilter($bool) {
    var elementsToTrigger = $([$('#cd-filter')]);
    elementsToTrigger.each(function () {
        $(this).toggleClass('filter-is-visible', $bool);
    });
}

var cartWrapper = $('.cd-cart-container');
var carturl = 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/cart/ajax';
var current_cart_campaigns = [];
var cart_session_id = false;
var cart_id = false;


/**
 *
 * Load Cart
 *
 */
$(document).on('ready', function () {

    //cart_session_id
    $.ajax({
        url: carturl,
        data: {
            'action': 'getCartSession',
            'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
        },
        type: 'post',
        datatype: 'json',
        success: function (output) {
            if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
                cart_session_id = output.cart_session_id;
                cart_id = output.cart_id;

                loadCart();

                $.ajax({
                    url: carturl,
                    data: {
                        'action': 'getExpiryCartSession',

                        'cart_session_id': cart_session_id,

                        'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
                    },
                    type: 'post',
                    datatype: 'json',
                    success: function (output) {
                        if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
                            if (output.status_id >= 3000) {
                                $.confirm({
                                    title: output.title,
                                    content: output.content,
                                    cancelButton: output.no_button,
                                    columnClass: 'col-md-offset-2 col-md-8',
                                    theme: 'black',
                                    autoClose: 'cancel|30000',
                                    buttons: {
                                        confirm: {
                                            text: output.yes_button,
                                            btnClass: 'btn-success',
                                            action: function () {
                                                $.ajax({
                                                    url: carturl,
                                                    data: {
                                                        'action': 'regenerateCartSession',

                                                        'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
                                                    },
                                                    type: 'post',
                                                    datatype: 'json',
                                                    success: function (output) {
                                                        if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
                                                            $.alert({
                                                                title: output.title,
                                                                content: output.content,
                                                                columnClass: 'col-md-offset-2 col-md-8'
                                                            });

                                                            cart_session_id = output.new_cart_session_id;
                                                            cart_id = output.new_cart_id;

                                                            loadCart();

                                                            quickUpdateCart();
                                                        } else {
                                                            $.alert({
                                                                onClose: function () {
                                                                    if (output.status_id == 1100) {
                                                                        $(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
                                                                    } else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
                                                                        location.reload(true);
                                                                    }
                                                                },
                                                                title: output.title,
                                                                content: output.content
                                                            });
                                                        }
                                                    },
                                                    error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                                                        /*$.alert({
                                                            content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                                                            title: "ERROR"
                                                        });*/
                                                    }
                                                });
                                            }
                                        },
                                        cancel: {
                                            text: output.no_button,
                                            btnClass: 'btn-danger',
                                            action: function () { //Regenerate cart session id
                                                $.ajax({ //Modif last_modified
                                                    url: carturl,
                                                    data: {
                                                        'action': 'prolongueCartSession',
                                                        'cart_session_id': cart_session_id,
                                                        'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
                                                    },
                                                    type: 'post',
                                                    datatype: 'json',
                                                    columnClass: 'col-md-offset-2 col-md-8',
                                                    success: function (output) {
                                                        if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
                                                            loadCart();
                                                        } else {
                                                            $.alert({
                                                                onClose: function () {
                                                                    if (output.status_id == 1100) {
                                                                        $(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
                                                                    } else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
                                                                        location.reload(true);
                                                                    }
                                                                },
                                                                title: output.title,
                                                                content: output.content
                                                            });
                                                        }
                                                    },
                                                    error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                                                        //$.alert({
                                                        //	content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                                                        //	title: "ERROR"
                                                        //});
                                                    }
                                                });
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                });
                            }
                        } else {
                            $.alert({
                                onClose: function () {
                                    if (output.status_id == 1100) {
                                        $(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
                                    } else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
                                        location.reload(true);
                                    }
                                },
                                title: output.title,
                                content: output.content
                            });
                        }
                    },
                    error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                        //$.alert({
                        //	content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                        //	title: "ERROR"
                        //});
                    }
                });
            } else {
                $.alert({
                    onClose: function () {
                        if (output.status_id == 1100) {
                            $(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
                        } else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
                            location.reload(true);
                        }
                    },
                    title: output.title,
                    content: output.content
                });
            }
        },
        error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
            //$.alert({
            //	content: "Unknown error has occured!",
            //	title: "ERROR"
            //});
        }
    });
});

if (cartWrapper.length > 0) {
    //store jQuery objects
    var cartBody = cartWrapper.find('.body')
    var cartList = cartBody.find('ul').eq(0);
    var cartTotal = cartWrapper.find('.checkout').find('span');
    var cartTrigger = cartWrapper.children('.cd-cart-trigger');
    var cartCount = cartTrigger.children('.count')
    var addToCartBtn = $('.cd-add-to-cart');
    var undo = cartWrapper.find('.undo');
    var undoTimeoutId;

    var currency = "RON";
    var maxCartAmount = "1500";

    var cartFilledTitle = "Cos Plin!";
    var cartFilledContent = "Cosul de cumparaturi este plin! Poti tine un maxim de %maxitems% produse!".replace("%maxitems%", maxCartAmount);

    var quantityLang = "Cant.";

    //add product to cart
    addToCartBtn.on('click', function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();

        if (cart_session_id) {
            addToCart($(this).data('pid'), $(this).data('price'), $(this).data('product-in-view-button'));
        } else {
            $.alert({
                content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                title: "ERROR"
            });
        }

    });

    //open/close cart
    cartTrigger.on('click', function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        toggleCart();
    });

    //close cart when clicking on the .cd-cart-container::before (bg layer)
    cartWrapper.on('click', function (event) {
        if ($(event.target).is($(this))) toggleCart(true);
    });

    //delete an item from the cart
    cartList.on('click', '.product-delete', function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();

        var cart_item_id = $(this).closest('div.product-details').data('cart-item-id');
        var cart_product_id = $(this).closest('div.product-details').data('product-id');


        var qtyHolder = $(this);
        var productHolder = $(event.target).parents('.cart-item');

        $.ajax({
            url: carturl,
            data: {
                'action': 'deleteCartItemModal',

                'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
            },
            type: 'post',
            datatype: 'json',
            success: function (output) {
                if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
                    $.confirm({
                        title: output.title,
                        content: output.content,
                        columnClass: 'col-md-offset-2 col-md-8',
                        theme: 'black',
                        buttons: {
                            confirm: {
                                text: output.yes_button,
                                btnClass: 'btn-success',
                                action: function () {
                                    $.ajax({
                                        url: carturl,
                                        data: {
                                            'action': 'deleteCartItem',

                                            'cart_session_id': cart_session_id,
                                            'cart_id': cart_id,
                                            'cart_item_id': cart_item_id,
                                            'cart_product_id': cart_product_id,

                                            'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
                                        },
                                        type: 'post',
                                        datatype: 'json',
                                        success: function (output) {
                                            if (output.status_id >= 2000 && output.status_id < 5200) {
                                                removeProduct(productHolder);

                                                loadCart();
                                            } else {
                                                $.alert({
                                                    onClose: function () {
                                                        if (output.status_id == 1100) {
                                                            $(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
                                                        } else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
                                                            location.reload(true);
                                                        }
                                                    },
                                                    title: output.title,
                                                    content: output.content
                                                });
                                            }
                                        },
                                        error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                                            $.alert({
                                                content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                                                title: "ERROR"
                                            });
                                        }
                                    });
                                }
                            },
                            cancel: {
                                text: output.no_button,
                                btnClass: 'btn-danger'
                            }
                        }
                    });
                } else {
                    $.alert({
                        onClose: function () {
                            if (output.status_id == 1100) {
                                $(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
                            } else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
                                location.reload(true);
                            }
                        },
                        title: output.title,
                        content: output.content
                    });
                }
            },
            error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                $.alert({
                    content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                    title: "ERROR"
                });
            }
        });
    });

    //update item quantity
    cartList.on('change', '.pQuantity', function (event) {
        $this = $(this);
        if ($(this).val() < 1) {
            if ($(this).data('prev') == undefined) {
                $(this).val(1);

                $(this).data('prev', 1);
            } else {
                $(this).val($(this).data('prev'));
            }
        } else {
            var qtyHolder = $(this);
            var newQuantity = $(qtyHolder).val();
            if ($.isNumeric(newQuantity) && (Math.floor(newQuantity) == newQuantity)) {
                $.ajax({
                    url: carturl,
                    data: {
                        'action': 'updateCartItem',

                        'cart_session_id': cart_session_id,
                        'cart_id': cart_id,
                        'cart_item_id': $(qtyHolder).closest('div.product-details').data('cart-item-id'),
                        'cart_product_id': $(qtyHolder).closest('div.product-details').data('product-id'),
                        'cart_item_new_quantity': $(qtyHolder).val(),

                        'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
                    },
                    type: 'post',
                    datatype: 'json',
                    success: function (output) {
                        if (output.status_id >= 2000 && output.status_id < 5200) {
                            $(qtyHolder).data('prev', $(qtyHolder).val());

                            quickUpdateCart($this);

                            if (output.reload_cart == 1) {
                                loadCart();
                            }
                        } else {
                            $.alert({
                                onClose: function () {
                                    if (output.status_id == 1100) {
                                        $(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
                                    } else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
                                        location.reload(true);
                                    }
                                },
                                title: output.title,
                                content: output.content
                            });

                            if ($(qtyHolder).data('prev') == undefined) {
                                $(qtyHolder).val(1);

                                $(qtyHolder).data('prev', 1);
                            } else {
                                $(qtyHolder).val($(qtyHolder).data('prev'));
                            }
                        }
                    },
                    error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                        $.alert({
                            content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                            title: "ERROR"
                        });

                        if ($(qtyHolder).data('prev') == undefined) {
                            $(qtyHolder).val(1);

                            $(qtyHolder).data('prev', 1);
                        } else {
                            $(qtyHolder).val($(qtyHolder).data('prev'));
                        }
                    }
                });
            } else {
                $(this).val($(this).data('prev'));
            }
        }
    });

function toggleCart(bool) {
    var cartIsOpen = (typeof bool === 'undefined') ? cartWrapper.hasClass('cart-open') : bool;

    if (cartIsOpen) {
        cartWrapper.removeClass('cart-open');

        clearInterval(undoTimeoutId);
        undo.removeClass('visible');
        cartList.find('.deleted').remove();

        setTimeout(function () {
            cartBody.scrollTop(0);

            if (Number(cartCount.find('li').eq(0).text()) == 0) cartWrapper.addClass('empty');
        }, 500);
    } else {
        cartWrapper.addClass('cart-open');
    }
}

function addToCart(productId, price, productPage) {
    if (getTotalQuantity() < maxCartAmount) {
        addProduct(productId, price, productPage);
    } else {
        cartFilledModal();
    }
}

function getTotalQuantity() {
    var quantity = 0;

    cartList.children('li:not(.deleted)').each(function () {
        quantity = quantity + Number($(this).find('.pQuantity').val());
    });

    return quantity;
}

function cartFilledModal() {
    $.alert({
        title: cartFilledTitle,
        content: cartFilledContent
    });
}

function loadCart() {
    if (cart_session_id && cart_id) {
        $.ajax({
            url: carturl,
            data: {
                'action': 'getCartContents',

                'cart_session_id': cart_session_id,
                'cart_id': cart_id,

                'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
            },
            type: 'post',
            datatype: 'json',
            columnClass: 'col-md-8',
            success: function (output) {
                if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
                    if (output.status_id == 2200) {
                        var show_message = "";

                        $.each(output.error_messages, function(i, error_message) {
                            show_message += error_message + "<br>";
                        });

                        $.alert({
                            title: "Ne pare rau dar cateva produse din cosul dvs. nu mai sunt disponibile in oferta noastra!",
                            content: show_message,
                            buttons: {
                                confirm: {
                                    text: "Am inteles!",
                                    btnClass: 'btn-success'
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    }

                    var cartIsEmpty = cartWrapper.hasClass('empty');
                    var totalPrice = 0;
                    var productCount = 0;
                    var i = 0;

                    cartList.empty();
                    $.each(output.cart_content, function (index, value) {
                        var ok = 0;
                        let attr_str = '';

                        $.each(value.t_attr_values, function (ind, val) {
                            if (val.t_attr_name != null) {
                                ok = 1;
                                attr_str += val.t_attr_name + ' - <strong>' + val.t_attr_value_name + '</strong>, ';
                            }
                        });

                        attr_str = attr_str.replace(/, +$/g, "");
                        value.t_products_image = value.t_products_image;
                        value.t_products_seo_path = "https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/" + value.t_products_seo_path;

                        //value.t_products_price = String(Number(value.t_products_price * value.t_quantity).toFixed(2));
                        var products_final_price = value.t_products_price;
                        products_final_price = Number(products_final_price).toFixed(2);
                        let products_final_price_to_show = Number(products_final_price*value.t_quantity).toFixed(2);

                        var price = products_final_price_to_show.split(".");

                        var minus_disabled = '';
                        if (value.t_quantity == 1) {
                            minus_disabled = 'disabled="disabled"';
                        }

                        var product_add = '<li class="cart-item"> \
								<div class="product-details" data-cart-item-id="' + value.t_item_id + '" data-product-id="' + value.t_products_id + '"> \
								<div class="row product-info"> \
								<div class="col-lg-2 col-md-2 hidden-sm hidden-xs product-image"> \
										<a class="product-img" href="' + value.t_products_seo_path + '" target="open_shop_product"> \
											<img src="' + value.t_products_image + '" class="img-responsive" alt="placeholder"> \
										</a> \
									</div> \
									<div class="col-lg-8 col-md-8 col-sm-10 col-xs-10"> \
										<h5><a href="' + value.t_products_seo_path + '" target="open_shop_product">' + value.t_products_name + '</a></h5>';
                        var priceToShow = '<span>' + price[0] + '<sup>' + price[1] + '</sup> ' + currency + '</span>';
                        if (value.t_products_price == 0.00) {
                            priceToShow = '<span class="text-success">Gratuit!</span>';
                        }

                        let products_price = '<span class="price">' + priceToShow + '</span>';

                        if ("t_campaign_product" in value) {
                            product_add += ' \
								<div class="input-group quantity col-xs-6"> \
									<input data-price="' + products_final_price + '" type="text" value="' + value.t_quantity + '" class="pQuantity form-control input-number imput-sm" DISABLED> \
								</div> \
							';
                        } else {
                            product_add += '<div class="input-group quantity col-xs-6"> \
													<span class="input-group-btn"> \
														<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-default btn-number" ' + minus_disabled + ' data-type="minus" data-field="quant[' + i + ']"> \
															<span class="glyphicon glyphicon-minus"></span> \
														</button> \
													</span> \
													<input data-price="' + products_final_price + '" type="text" name="quant[' + i + ']" class="pQuantity form-control input-number input-sm" \
																value="' + value.t_quantity + '" min="1" max="' + maxCartAmount + '" data-prev="' + value.t_quantity + '"> \
													<span class="input-group-btn"> \
														<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-default btn-number" data-type="plus" data-field="quant[' + i + ']"> \
															<span class="glyphicon glyphicon-plus"></span> \
														</button> \
													</span> \
												</div>';
                        }

                        product_add += '<div class="col-xs-6">' + products_price + '</div>';

                        product_add += '</div> \
									<div class="col-lg-2 col-md-2 col-sm-2 col-xs-2 delete-action">';

                        if (!("t_campaign_product" in value)) {
                            product_add += '<a href="#0" class="product-delete pull-right"><i class="fa fa-close fa-2x"></i></a>';
                        }

                        product_add += '</div>';
                        product_add += '</div>';
                        if (ok == 1) {
                            product_add += '<div class="row product-attributes"><div class="col-lg-12 col-md-12 col-sm-12 col-xs-12"> \
									' + attr_str + ' \
								</div></div>';
                        }
                        product_add += '</div></div> \
							<div class="row separator"></div> \
						</li>';

                        var productAdded = $(product_add);
                        cartList.prepend(productAdded);
                        i++;

                        cartWrapper.removeClass('empty');
                        if (!("t_campaign_product" in value)) {
                            totalPrice = Number(totalPrice) + Number(products_final_price) * Number(value.t_quantity);
                            productCount = Number(productCount) + Number(value.t_quantity);
                        }
                    });

                    if (output.cart_campaign.length > 0) {
                        var campaign_add = '<div class="row cart-campaigns-title text-bold"><div class="col-lg-12 col-md-12 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">Campanii aplicate pe cos</div></div>';
                        current_cart_campaigns = output.cart_campaign;

                        $.each(output.cart_campaign, function (index, value) {
                            if (typeof value.text !== "undefined") {
                                campaign_add += '<div class="row cart-campaign"><div class="col-lg-12 col-md-12 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">- ' + value.text + '</div></div>';

                                if ("benefits" in value) {
                                    $.each(value.benefits, function(index, value) {
                                        if ("total_price_reduction" in value) {
                                            totalPrice -= value.total_price_reduction;
                                        }
                                    });
                                }
                            }
                        });
                        var campaignAdded = $(campaign_add);
                        cartList.append(campaignAdded);
                    } else {
                        current_cart_campaigns = [];
                    }

                    cartCount.find('li').eq(0).text(productCount);
                    cartCount.find('li').eq(1).text(productCount + 1);

                    if (totalPrice < 0) {
                        totalPrice = 0;
                    }

                    updateCartTotal(totalPrice, true);
                    quickUpdateCart();
                } else {
                    $.alert({
                        onClose: function () {
                            if (output.status_id == 1100) {
                                $(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
                            } else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
                                location.reload(true);
                            }
                        },
                        title: output.title,
                        content: output.content
                    });
                }
            },
            error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                //$.alert({
                //	content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                //	title: "ERROR"
                //});
            }
        });
    }
}

function addProduct(productId, price, productPage) {
    var ok = 1;
    var i = 0;

    var attrArray = {};

    if (productPage) {
        $('select.attr').each(function (index) {
            var selectedValue = $(this).find('option:selected').val();

            var attr_id = $(this).data('attr-id');
            var attr_value_id = $(this).find('option:selected').data('attr-value-id');

            attrArray[i] = {};
            attrArray[i]['attr_id'] = attr_id;
            attrArray[i]['attr_value_id'] = attr_value_id;

            i++;

            if (selectedValue == -1) {
                $(this).parents('div.form-group').addClass('has-error');
                ok = 0;
            } else {
                $(this).parents('div.form-group').removeClass('has-error');
            }
        });
    }

    if (ok == 1) {
        $('#attributes-panel').removeClass('panel-danger').addClass('panel-primary');

        var products_quantity = 1;

        if ($('#addToCartQuantity').length > 0) {
            products_quantity = $('#addToCartQuantity').val()
        }

        $.ajax({
            url: carturl,
            data: {
                'action': 'addToCart',

                'cart_session_id': cart_session_id,
                'cart_id': cart_id,
                'product_id': productId,
                'attr': JSON.stringify(attrArray),
                'product_quantity': products_quantity,

                'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
            },
            type: 'post',
            datatype: 'json',
            success: function (output) {
                if (output.status_id >= 2000 && output.status_id < 5200) {
                    loadCart();
                } else {
                    if (output.status_id == 1400) {
                        var show_message = "";

                        $.each(output.error_messages, function(i, error_message) {
                            show_message += error_message + "<br>";
                        });

                        $.alert({
                            title: "A aparut o eroare!",
                            content: show_message,
                            buttons: {
                                confirm: {
                                    text: "Am inteles!",
                                    btnClass: 'btn-success'
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    } else {
                        $.alert({
                            onClose: function () {
                                if (output.status_id == 1100) {
                                    $(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
                                } else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
                                    location.reload(true);
                                }
                            },
                            title: output.title,
                            content: output.content
                        });
                    }
                }
            },
            error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                $.alert({
                    content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                    title: "ERROR"
                });
            }
        });
    } else {
        $('#attributes-panel').removeClass('panel-primary').addClass('panel-danger');

        $.alert({
            title: "Alegeti un model!",
            content: "Produsul pe care doriti sa il adaugati in cos necesita alegerea unui model!",
            buttons: {
                confirm: {
                    text: "Ok",
                    btnClass: 'btn-success'
                }
            }
        });
    }
}

function removeProduct(product) {
    clearInterval(undoTimeoutId);
    cartList.find('.deleted').remove();
    $qty = product.find('.pQuantity');

    var topPosition = product.offset().top - cartBody.children('ul').offset().top,
        productQuantity = Number($qty.val()),
        productTotPrice = Number($qty.data('price')) * productQuantity;

    product.css('top', topPosition + 'px').addClass('deleted');

    updateCartTotal(productTotPrice, false);
    updateCartCount(true, -productQuantity);
    undo.addClass('visible');

    undoTimeoutId = setTimeout(function () {
        undo.removeClass('visible');
        cartList.find('.deleted').remove();
    }, 8000);
}

function quickUpdateCart($element = null) {
    var quantity = 0;
    var newTotal = 0;
    var numberOfDifferentProducts = 0;

    if ($element) {
        let qty = Number($element.val());
        let newLineTotal = (qty * Number($element.data('price'))).toFixed(2).split(".");
        $element.closest('div.product-info').find('span.price>span').html(newLineTotal[0] + '<sup>' + newLineTotal[1] + '</sup> ' + currency);
    }

    cartList.children('li:not(.deleted)').each(function () {
        $qty = $(this).find('.pQuantity');
        var singleQuantity = Number($qty.val());
        quantity = quantity + singleQuantity;
        newTotal = newTotal + singleQuantity * Number($qty.data('price'));

        numberOfDifferentProducts += 1;
    });
    
    if (current_cart_campaigns.length > 0) {
        $.each(current_cart_campaigns, function (index, value) {
            if (value.isApplied == 1) {
                if ("benefits" in value) {
                    $.each(value.benefits, function(index, value) {
                        if ("total_price_reduction" in value) {
                            newTotal -= value.total_price_reduction;
                        }
                    });
                }
            }
        });
    }

    if (numberOfDifferentProducts == 0) {
        cartWrapper.removeClass('cart-open');
        cartWrapper.addClass('empty');
    } else {
        cartWrapper.removeClass('empty');
    }

    if (quantity <= maxCartAmount) {
        newTotal = newTotal.toFixed(2);
        cartTotal.data('total', newTotal);
        newTotal = newTotal.split(".");

        cartTotal.html(newTotal[0] + '<sup>' + newTotal[1] + '</sup> ');

        cartCount.find('li').eq(0).text(quantity);
        cartCount.find('li').eq(1).text(quantity + 1);

        return true;
    }

    return false;
}

function updateCartCount(emptyCart, quantity) {
    if (typeof quantity === 'undefined') {
        var actual = Number(cartCount.find('li').eq(0).text()) + 1;
        var next = actual + 1;

        if (emptyCart) {
            cartCount.find('li').eq(0).text(actual);
            cartCount.find('li').eq(1).text(next);
        } else {
            cartCount.addClass('update-count');

            setTimeout(function () {
                cartCount.find('li').eq(0).text(actual);
            }, 150);

            setTimeout(function () {
                cartCount.removeClass('update-count');
            }, 200);

            setTimeout(function () {
                cartCount.find('li').eq(1).text(next);
            }, 230);
        }
    } else {
        var actual = Number(cartCount.find('li').eq(0).text()) + quantity;
        var next = actual + 1;

        cartCount.find('li').eq(0).text(actual);
        cartCount.find('li').eq(1).text(next);
    }
}

function updateCartTotal(price, bool) {
    var newTotal = 0;
    var currentTotal = Number(cartTotal.data('total'));
    price = Number(price);

    if (bool) {
        newTotal = (currentTotal + price);
    } else {
        newTotal = (currentTotal - price);
    }
    cartTotal.data('total', newTotal);

    newTotal = newTotal.toFixed(2).split(".");

    cartTotal.html(newTotal[0] + '<sup>' + newTotal[1] + '</sup> ');
}

$(document).on('click', '.btn-number', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    var fieldName = $(this).attr('data-field');
    var type = $(this).attr('data-type');
    var input = $("input[name='" + fieldName + "']");
    var currentVal = parseInt(input.val());
    if (!isNaN(currentVal)) {
        if (type == 'minus') {

            if (currentVal > input.attr('min')) {
                input.val(currentVal - 1).change();
            }
            if (parseInt(input.val()) == input.attr('min')) {
                $(this).attr('disabled', true);
            }

        } else if (type == 'plus') {

            if (currentVal < input.attr('max')) {
                input.val(currentVal + 1).change();
            }
            if (parseInt(input.val()) == input.attr('max')) {
                $(this).attr('disabled', true);
            }

        }
    } else {
        input.val(0);
    }
});

$(document).on('focusin', '.input-number', function () {
    $(this).data('oldValue', $(this).val());
});

$(document).on('change', '.input-number', function () {
    var minValue = parseInt($(this).attr('min'));
    var maxValue = parseInt($(this).attr('max'));
    var valueCurrent = parseInt($(this).val());

    var name = $(this).attr('name');
    if (valueCurrent >= minValue) {
        $(".btn-number[data-type='minus'][data-field='" + name + "']").removeAttr('disabled')
    } else {
        $(this).val($(this).data('oldValue'));
    }
    if (valueCurrent <= maxValue) {
        $(".btn-number[data-type='plus'][data-field='" + name + "']").removeAttr('disabled')
    } else {
        $(this).val($(this).data('oldValue'));
    }
});

$(document).on('keyDown', ".input-number", function (e) {
    if ($.inArray(e.keyCode, [46, 8, 9, 27, 13, 190]) !== -1 ||
        (e.keyCode == 65 && e.ctrlKey === true) ||
        (e.keyCode >= 35 && e.keyCode <= 39)) {
        return;
    }

    if ((e.shiftKey || (e.keyCode < 48 || e.keyCode > 57)) && (e.keyCode < 96 || e.keyCode > 105)) {
        e.preventDefault();
    }
});

var wishlistWrapper = $('.cd-wishlist-container');
var wishlisturl = 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/wishlist/ajax';

var wishlist_id = false;
var wishlist_name = false;

 * Load Wishlist

	loadWishlist();
});

function loadWishlist() {
	$.ajax({
		url: wishlisturl,
		data: {
			'action': 'getWishlistSession',

			'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
		},
		type: 'post',
		datatype: 'json',
		success: function (output) {
			if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
				wishlist_session_id = output.wishlist_session_id;
				wishlist_id = output.wishlist_id;
				wishlist_name = output.wishlist_name;

				loadWishlistContent();
			} else {
				$.alert({
					onClose: function () {
						if (output.status_id == 1100) {
							$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
						} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
							location.reload(true);
						}
					},
					title: output.title,
					content: output.content
				});
			}
		},
		error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
			//$.alert({
			//	content: "Unknown error has occured!",
			//	title: "ERROR"
			//});
		}
	});
}

	$(document).on('click', '.exchange-wl-sc', function () {
		var wishlist_id = $('.list-left ul').data('wishlist-id');
		var cart_id = $('.list-right ul').data('cart-id');
		var wishlist_items = $('.list-left ul li.list-group-item-info');
		var cart_items = $('.list-right ul li.list-group-item-info');

		var wishlistArray = [];
		var cartArray = [];

		$.each(wishlist_items, function (index, item) {
			var item_id = $(item).data('item-id');

			wishlistArray.push(item_id);
		});

		$.each(cart_items, function (index, item) {
			var item_id = $(item).data('item-id');

			cartArray.push(item_id);
		});

		$.ajax({
			url: wishlisturl,
			data: {
				'action': 'wishlistToCartTransfer',

				'wishlist_id': wishlist_id,
				'cart_id': cart_id,
				'wishlistArray': JSON.stringify(wishlistArray),
				'cartArray': JSON.stringify(cartArray),

				'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
			},
			type: 'post',
			datatype: 'json',
			success: function (output) {
				if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
					cart_items.clone().appendTo('.list-left ul');
					cart_items.remove();

					wishlist_items.clone().appendTo('.list-right ul');
					wishlist_items.remove();

					$('.product-transfer ul li.list-group-item-info span').removeClass('fa-check-square-o').addClass('fa-square-o');
					$('.product-transfer ul li.list-group-item-info').removeClass('list-group-item-info');

					loadWishlistContent();
					loadCart();

					toggleWishlist(true);
					wishlistTransferModal.close();
				} else {
					$.alert({
						onClose: function () {
							if (output.status_id == 1100) {
								$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
							} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
								location.reload(true);
							}

						title: output.title,
						content: output.content
					});

			},
			error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
				$.alert({
					content: "Unknown error has occured!",
					title: "ERROR"
				});
			}
		});
	});

	$(document).on('click', '.dual-list .selector', function () {
		var $checkBox = $(this);
		if (!$checkBox.hasClass('selected')) {
			$checkBox.closest('.well').find('ul li:not(.list-group-item-info) span').removeClass('fa-square-o').addClass('fa-check-square-o');
			$checkBox.addClass('selected').closest('.well').find('ul li:not(.list-group-item-info)').addClass('list-group-item-info');
		} else {
			$checkBox.closest('.well').find('ul li.list-group-item-info span').removeClass('fa-check-square-o').addClass('fa-square-o');
			$checkBox.removeClass('selected').closest('.well').find('ul li.list-group-item-info').removeClass('list-group-item-info');
		}
	});

	$(document).on('keyup', '[name="productSearch"]', function (e) {
		var code = e.keyCode || e.which;
		if (code == '9') return;
		if (code == '27') $(this).val(null);

		var val = $.trim($(this).val()).replace(/ +/g, ' ').toLowerCase();
		var $rows = $(this).closest('.dual-list').find('.list-group li');

		$rows.show().filter(function () {
			var text = $(this).text().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').toLowerCase();
			return !~text.indexOf(val);
		}).hide();
	});

	// --------------------------------------------------------------------- Wishlist Renaming System
	var isRenaming = false;
	var initialName;

	function switchWishlistNameToInput(callingObject) {
		var wishlistNameObject = callingObject.parent('header').children('h2').children('span');
		var replaceObject = $('<input id="wishlist-rename" />').val(wishlistNameObject.html());

		initialName = wishlistNameObject.html();

		wishlistNameObject.replaceWith(replaceObject);

		isRenaming = true;
	}

	function saveNewWishlistName(callingObject) {
		$.ajax({
			url: wishlisturl,
			data: {
				'action': 'renameWishlist',

				'wishlist_id': wishlist_id,
				'wishlist_name': callingObject.val(),

				'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
			},
			type: 'post',
			datatype: 'json',
			success: function (output) {
				var newObject;

				if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
					newObject = $('<span id="wishlist-name" class="wishlist-name">' + callingObject.val() + '</span>');
				} else {
					$.alert({
						onClose: function () {
							if (output.status_id == 1100) {
								$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
							} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
								location.reload(true);
							}
						},
						title: output.title,
						content: output.content
					});

					newObject = $('<span id="wishlist-name" class="wishlist-name">' + initialName + '</span>');
				}

				callingObject.replaceWith(newObject);

				isRenaming = false;
			},
			error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
				$.alert({
					content: "Unknown error has occured!",
					title: "ERROR"
				});
			}
		});
	}

		e.preventDefault();

			switchWishlistNameToInput($(this));

			isRenaming = false;
		}
	});

										datatype: 'json',
										success: function (output) {
											if (output.status_id >= 2000 && output.status_id < 5200) {
												loadWishlist();

												undo.removeClass('visible');
												wishlistList.find('.deleted').remove();

												wishlistWrapper.removeClass('wishlist-open');

												setTimeout(function () {
													wishlistBody.scrollTop(0);

													if (Number(wishlistCount.find('li').eq(0).text()) == 0) wishlistWrapper.addClass('empty');
												}, 500);

												if (showAllWishlistsModal != undefined) {
													showAllWishlistsModal.close();
												}
											} else {
												$.alert({
													onClose: function () {
														if (output.status_id == 1100) {
															$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
														} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
															location.reload(true);
														}
													},
													title: output.title,
													content: output.content
												});
											}
										},
										error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
											$.alert({
												content: "Unknown error has occured!",
												title: "ERROR"
											});
										}
									});
								}
							},
							cancel: {
								text: output.no_button,
								btnClass: 'btn-danger'
							}
						}
					});
				} else {
					$.alert({
						onClose: function () {
							if (output.status_id == 1100) {
								$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
							} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
								location.reload(true);
							}
						},
						title: output.title,
						content: output.content
					});
				}
			},
			error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
				$.alert({
					content: "Unknown error has occured!",
					title: "ERROR"
				});
			}
		});
	});

	$(document).on('click', '#switchActiveWishlist', function (e) {
		e.preventDefault();

		var new_active_wishlist_id = $(this).data('wishlist-id');

		$.ajax({
			url: wishlisturl,
			data: {
				'action': 'confirmWishlistSwitchModal',

				'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
			},
			type: 'post',
			datatype: 'json',
			success: function (output) {
				if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
					$.confirm({
						title: output.title,
						content: output.content,
						columnClass: 'col-md-offset-4 col-md-4',
						theme: 'black',
						buttons: {
							confirm: {
								text: output.yes_button,
								btnClass: 'btn-success',
								action: function () {
									$.ajax({
										url: wishlisturl,
										data: {
											'action': 'switchActiveWishlist',

											'new_active_wishlist_id': new_active_wishlist_id,

											'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
										},
										type: 'post',
										datatype: 'json',
										success: function (output) {
											if (output.status_id >= 2000 && output.status_id < 5200) {
												loadWishlist();

												undo.removeClass('visible');
												wishlistList.find('.deleted').remove();

												wishlistWrapper.removeClass('wishlist-open');

												setTimeout(function () {
													wishlistBody.scrollTop(0);

													if (Number(wishlistCount.find('li').eq(0).text()) == 0) wishlistWrapper.addClass('empty');
												}, 500);

												if (showAllWishlistsModal != undefined) {
													showAllWishlistsModal.close();
												}
											} else {
												$.alert({
													onClose: function () {
														if (output.status_id == 1100) {
															$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
														} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
															location.reload(true);
														}
													},
													title: output.title,
													content: output.content
												});
											}
										},
										error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
											$.alert({
												content: "Unknown error has occured!",
												title: "ERROR"
											});
										}
									});
								}
							},
							cancel: {
								text: output.no_button,
								btnClass: 'btn-danger'
							}
						}
					});
				} else {
					$.alert({
						onClose: function () {
							if (output.status_id == 1100) {
								$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
							} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
								location.reload(true);
							}
						},
						title: output.title,
						content: output.content
					});
				}
			},
			error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
				$.alert({
					content: "Unknown error has occured!",
					title: "ERROR"
				});
			}
		});
	});

	//delete an item from the wishlist
	wishlistList.on('click', '.product-delete', function (event) {
		event.preventDefault();

		var wishlist_item_id = $(this).closest('div.product-details').data('wishlist-item-id');
		var wishlist_product_id = $(this).closest('div.product-details').data('product-id');


		var qtyHolder = $(this);
		var productHolder = $(event.target).parents('.wishlist-item');

		$.ajax({
			url: wishlisturl,
			data: {
				'action': 'deleteWishlistItemModal',

				'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
			},
			type: 'post',
			datatype: 'json',
			success: function (output) {
				if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
					$.confirm({
						title: output.title,
						content: output.content,
						columnClass: 'col-md-8 col-md-offset-2',
						theme: 'black',
						buttons: {
							confirm: {
								text: output.yes_button,
								btnClass: 'btn-success',
								action: function () {
									$.ajax({
										url: wishlisturl,
										data: {
											'action': 'deleteWishlistItem',

											'wishlist_session_id': wishlist_session_id,
											'wishlist_id': wishlist_id,
											'wishlist_item_id': wishlist_item_id,
											'wishlist_product_id': wishlist_product_id,

											'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
										},
										type: 'post',
										datatype: 'json',
										success: function (output) {
											if (output.status_id >= 2000 && output.status_id < 5200) {
												removeWishlistProduct(productHolder);
											} else {
												if (output.new_session_id != undefined && output.new_id != undefined && output.new_name != undefined) {
													wishlist_session_id = output.new_session_id;
													wishlist_id = output.new_id;
													wishlist_name = output.new_name;
												}

												$.alert({
													onClose: function () {
														if (output.status_id == 1100) {
															$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
														} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
															location.reload(true);
														}
													},
													title: output.title,
													content: output.content
												});
											}
										},
										error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
											$.alert({
												content: "Unknown error has occured!",
												title: "ERROR"
											});
										}
									});
								}
							},
							cancel: {
								text: output.no_button,
								btnClass: 'btn-danger'
							}
						}
					});
				} else {
					$.alert({
						onClose: function () {
							if (output.status_id == 1100) {
								$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
							} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
								location.reload(true);
							}
						},
						title: output.title,
						content: output.content
					});
				}
			},
			error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
				$.alert({
					content: "Unknown error has occured!",
					title: "ERROR"
				});
			}
		});
	});

	//update item quantity
	wishlistList.on('change', '.pQuantity', function (event) {
        $this = $(this);
		if ($(this).val() < 1) {
			if ($(this).data('prev') == undefined) {
				$(this).val(1);

				$(this).data('prev', 1);
			} else {
				$(this).val($(this).data('prev'));
			}
		} else {
			var qtyHolder = $(this);
			var newQuantity = $(qtyHolder).val();
			if ($.isNumeric(newQuantity) && (Math.floor(newQuantity) == newQuantity)) {
				$.ajax({
					url: wishlisturl,
					data: {
						'action': 'updateWishlistItem',

						'wishlist_session_id': wishlist_session_id,
						'wishlist_id': wishlist_id,
						'wishlist_item_id': $(qtyHolder).closest('div.product-details').data('wishlist-item-id'),
						'wishlist_product_id': $(qtyHolder).closest('div.product-details').data('product-id'),
						'wishlist_item_new_quantity': $(qtyHolder).val(),

						'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
					},
					type: 'post',
					datatype: 'json',
					success: function (output) {
						if (output.status_id >= 2000 && output.status_id < 5200) {
							$(qtyHolder).data('prev', $(qtyHolder).val());

							quickUpdateWishlist($this);
						} else {
							if (output.new_session_id != undefined && output.new_id != undefined && output.new_name != undefined) {
								wishlist_session_id = output.new_session_id;
								wishlist_id = output.new_id;
								wishlist_name = output.new_name;
							}

							$.alert({
								onClose: function () {
									if (output.status_id == 1100) {
										$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
									} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
										location.reload(true);
									}
								},
								title: output.title,
								content: output.content
							});

							if ($(qtyHolder).data('prev') == undefined) {
								$(qtyHolder).val(1);

								$(qtyHolder).data('prev', 1);
							} else {
								$(qtyHolder).val($(qtyHolder).data('prev'));
							}
						}
					},
					error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
						//$.alert({
						//	content: "Unknown error has occured!",
						//	title: "ERROR"
						//});

						if ($(qtyHolder).data('prev') == undefined) {
							$(qtyHolder).val(1);

							$(qtyHolder).data('prev', 1);
						} else {
							$(qtyHolder).val($(qtyHolder).data('prev'));
						}
					}
				});
			} else {
				$(this).val($(this).data('prev'));
			}
		}
	});


	//reinsert item deleted from the wishlist
	undo.on('click', 'a', function (event) {
		clearInterval(undoTimeoutId);
		event.preventDefault();

		wishlistList.find('.deleted').addClass('undo-deleted').one('webkitAnimationEnd oanimationend msAnimationEnd animationend',
			function () {
				var currentProduct = $(this);
				var productHolder = $(this).find('div.product-details');

				$.ajax({
					url: wishlisturl,
					data: {
						'action': 'activateWishlistItem',

						'wishlist_session_id': wishlist_session_id,
						'wishlist_id': wishlist_id,
						'wishlist_item_id': productHolder.data('wishlist-item-id'),
						'wishlist_product_id': productHolder.data('product-id'),

						'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
					},
					type: 'post',
					datatype: 'json',
					success: function (output) {
						if (output.status_id >= 2000 && output.status_id < 5200) {
							$(currentProduct).off('webkitAnimationEnd oanimationend msAnimationEnd animationend')
								.removeClass('deleted undo-deleted')
								.removeAttr('style');

							quickUpdateWishlist();
							undo.removeClass('visible');
						} else {
							$.alert({
								onClose: function () {
									if (output.status_id == 1100) {
										$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
									} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
										location.reload(true);
									}
								},
								title: output.title,
								content: output.content
							});
						}
					},
					error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
						$.alert({
							content: "Unknown error has occured!",
							title: "ERROR"
						});
					}
				});
			});
	});
}

function toggleWishlist(bool) {
	var wishlistIsOpen = (typeof bool === 'undefined') ? wishlistWrapper.hasClass('wishlist-open') : bool;

	if (wishlistIsOpen) {
		wishlistWrapper.removeClass('wishlist-open');
		undo.removeClass('visible');
		
		clearInterval(undoTimeoutId);

		wishlistList.find('.deleted').remove();
		
		setTimeout(function () {
			wishlistBody.scrollTop(0);

			if (Number(wishlistCount.find('li').eq(0).text()) == 0) wishlistWrapper.addClass('empty');
		}, 500);
	} else {
		wishlistWrapper.addClass('wishlist-open');
	}
}

function addToWishlist(productId, price, productPage) {
	if (getTotalQuantity() < maxWishlistAmount) {
		addWishlistProduct(productId, price, productPage);
	} else {
		wishlistFilledModal();
	}
}

function getTotalQuantity() {
	var quantity = 0;

	wishlistList.children('li:not(.deleted)').each(function () {
		quantity = quantity + Number($(this).find('.pQuantity').val());
	});

	return quantity;
}

function wishlistFilledModal() {
	$.alert({
		title: wishlistFilledTitle,
		content: wishlistFilledContent
	});
}

function loadWishlistContent() {
	if (wishlist_session_id && wishlist_id && wishlist_name) {
		wishlistNameWrapper.html(wishlist_name);

		$.ajax({
			url: wishlisturl,
			data: {
				'action': 'getWishlistContents',

				'wishlist_session_id': wishlist_session_id,
				'wishlist_id': wishlist_id,

				'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
			},
			type: 'post',
			datatype: 'json',
			columnClass: 'col-md-8 col-md-offset-2',
			success: function (output) {
				if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
					if (output.new_session_id != undefined && output.new_id != undefined && output.new_name != undefined) {
						wishlist_session_id = output.new_session_id;
						wishlist_id = output.new_id;
						wishlist_name = output.new_name;
					}

					var totalPrice = 0;
					var productCount = 0;
					var i = 199;

					wishlistList.empty();
					$.each(output.wishlist_content, function (index, value) {
						var ok = 0;
						let attr_str = '';

						$.each(value.t_attr_values, function(ind, val) {
							if(val.t_attr_name != null) {
								ok = 1;
								attr_str += val.t_attr_name + ' - <strong>' + val.t_attr_value_name + '</strong>, ';
							}
						});

						attr_str = attr_str.replace(/, +$/g,"");

						value.t_products_image = value.t_products_image;
						value.t_products_seo_path = "https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/" + value.t_products_seo_path;

						//value.t_products_price = String(Number(value.t_products_price * value.t_quantity).toFixed(2));
						var products_final_price = value.t_products_price;
						products_final_price = Number(products_final_price).toFixed(2);
                        let products_final_price_to_show = Number(products_final_price*value.t_quantity).toFixed(2);

						var price = products_final_price_to_show.split(".");

						var minus_disabled = '';
						if(value.t_quantity == 1) {
							minus_disabled = 'disabled="disabled"';
						}

						var product_add = '<li class="wishlist-item"> \
							<div class="product-details" data-wishlist-item-id="' + value.t_item_id + '" data-product-id="' + value.t_products_id + '"> \
								<div class="row product-info"> \
								<div class="col-lg-2 col-md-2 hidden-sm hidden-xs product-image"> \
										<a class="product-img" href="' + value.t_products_seo_path + '" target="open_shop_product"> \
											<img src="' + value.t_products_image + '" class="img-responsive" alt="placeholder"> \
										</a> \
									</div> \
									<div class="col-lg-8 col-md-8 col-sm-10 col-xs-10"> \
										<h5><a href="' + value.t_products_seo_path + '" target="open_shop_product">' + value.t_products_name + '</a></h5>';
                        var priceToShow = '<span>' + price[0] + '<sup>' + price[1] + '</sup> ' + currency + '</span>';
						if (value.t_products_price == 0.00) {
							priceToShow = "<font color='green'>Gratis!</font>";
						}

						let products_price = '<span class="price" data-price="' + (value.t_products_price - value.t_products_discount) + '">' + priceToShow + '</span>';

						product_add += '<div class="input-group quantity col-xs-5"> \
													<span class="input-group-btn"> \
														<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-default btn-number" ' + minus_disabled + ' data-type="minus" data-field="quant[' + i + ']"> \
															<span class="glyphicon glyphicon-minus"></span> \
														</button> \
													</span> \
													<input data-price="' + products_final_price + '" type="text" name="quant[' + i + ']" class="pQuantity form-control input-number input-sm" \
																value="' + value.t_quantity + '" min="1" max="' + maxCartAmount + '" data-prev="' + value.t_quantity + '"> \
													<span class="input-group-btn"> \
														<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-default btn-number" data-type="plus" data-field="quant[' + i + ']"> \
															<span class="glyphicon glyphicon-plus"></span> \
														</button> \
													</span> \
												</div>';
						product_add += '<div class="col-xs-7">' + products_price + '</div>';

						product_add += '</div> \
									<div class="col-lg-2 col-md-2 col-sm-2 col-xs-2 delete-action">';

						if (!("t_campaign_product" in value)) {
							product_add += '<a href="#0" class="product-delete pull-right"><i class="fa fa-close fa-2x"></i></a>';
						}

						product_add += '</div>';
						product_add += '</div>';
						if (ok == 1) {
							product_add += '<div class="row product-attributes"><div class="col-lg-12 col-md-12 col-sm-12 col-xs-12"> \
									' + attr_str + ' \
								</div></div>';
						}
						product_add += '</div></div> \
							<div class="row separator"></div> \
						</li>';

						var productAdded = $(product_add);
						wishlistList.prepend(productAdded);

						wishlistWrapper.removeClass('empty');
						totalPrice = Number(totalPrice) + Number(value.t_products_price) * Number(value.t_quantity);
						productCount = Number(productCount) + Number(value.t_quantity);

						i++;
					});

					wishlistCount.find('li').eq(0).text(productCount);
					wishlistCount.find('li').eq(1).text(productCount + 1);

					quickUpdateWishlist();
				} else {
					$.alert({
						onClose: function () {
							if (output.status_id == 1100) {
								$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
							} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
								location.reload(true);
							}
						},
						title: output.title,
						content: output.content
					});
				}
			},
			error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
				//$.alert({
				//	content: "Unknown error has occured!",
				//	title: "ERROR"
				//});
			}
		});
	}
}

function addWishlistProduct(productId, price, productPage) {
	var ok = 1;
	var i = 0;

	var attrArray = {};

	if (productPage) {
		$('select.attr').each(function (index) {
			var selectedValue = $(this).find('option:selected').val();

			var attr_id = $(this).data('attr-id');
			var attr_value_id = $(this).find('option:selected').data('attr-value-id');

			attrArray[i] = {};
			attrArray[i]['attr_id'] = attr_id;
			attrArray[i]['attr_value_id'] = attr_value_id;

			i++;

			if (selectedValue == -1) {
				$(this).parents('div.form-group').addClass('has-error');
				ok = 0;
			} else {
				$(this).parents('div.form-group').removeClass('has-error');
			}
		});

		// update quantity amount
		if (ok == 1) {
			$('.cd-add-to-wishlist[data-product-in-view-button="true"]').html(parseInt($('.cd-add-to-wishlist[data-product-in-view-button="true"]').html()) + 1);
		}
	}

	if (ok == 1) {
		$('#attributes-panel').removeClass('panel-danger').addClass('panel-primary');
		$.ajax({
			url: wishlisturl,
			data: {
				'action': 'addToWishlist',

				'wishlist_session_id': wishlist_session_id,
				'wishlist_id': wishlist_id,
				'product_id': productId,
				'attr': JSON.stringify(attrArray),
				// 'product_quantity':      1, <- za future is here

				'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
			},
			type: 'post',
			datatype: 'json',
			success: function (output) {
				if (output.status_id >= 2000 && output.status_id < 5200) {
					loadWishlistContent();
				} else {
					$.alert({
						onClose: function () {
							if (output.status_id == 1100) {
								$(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
							} else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
								location.reload(true);
							}
						},
						title: output.title,
						content: output.content
					});
				}
			},
			error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
				$.alert({
					content: "Unknown error has occured!",
					title: "ERROR"
				});
			}
		});
	} else {
		$('#attributes-panel').removeClass('panel-primary').addClass('panel-danger');
	}
}

function removeWishlistProduct(product) {
	clearInterval(undoTimeoutId);
	wishlistList.find('.deleted').remove();

	var topPosition = product.offset().top - wishlistBody.children('ul').offset().top,
		productQuantity = Number(product.find('.quantity').find('.pQuantity').val()),
		productTotPrice = Number(product.find('span.price').data('price')) * productQuantity;

	product.css('top', topPosition + 'px').addClass('deleted');

	updatewishlistCount(true, -productQuantity);
	undo.addClass('visible');

	undoTimeoutId = setTimeout(function () {
		undo.removeClass('visible');
		wishlistList.find('.deleted').remove();
	}, 8000);
}

function quickUpdateWishlist($element) {
	var quantity = 0;
	var newTotal = 0;

    if ($element) {
        let qty = Number($element.val());
        let newLineTotal = (qty * Number($element.data('price'))).toFixed(2).split(".");
        $element.closest('div.product-info').find('span.price>span').html(newLineTotal[0] + '<sup>' + newLineTotal[1] + '</sup> ' + currency);
    }

	wishlistList.children('li:not(.deleted)').each(function () {
		var singleQuantity = Number($(this).find('.pQuantity').val());
		quantity = quantity + singleQuantity;
		newTotal = newTotal + singleQuantity * Number($(this).find('span.price').data('price'));
	});

	if (newTotal == 0) {
		cartWrapper.removeClass('wishlist-open');
		wishlistWrapper.addClass('empty');
	} else {
		wishlistWrapper.removeClass('empty');
	}

	if (quantity <= maxWishlistAmount) {
		newTotal = newTotal.toFixed(2);
		wishlistTotal.data('total', newTotal);
		newTotal = newTotal.split(".");

		wishlistTotal.html(newTotal[0] + '<sup>' + newTotal[1] + '</sup> ');

		wishlistCount.find('li').eq(0).text(quantity);
		wishlistCount.find('li').eq(1).text(quantity + 1);

		return true;
	}

	return false;
}

function updatewishlistCount(emptyWishlist, quantity) {
	if (typeof quantity === 'undefined') {
		var actual = Number(wishlistCount.find('li').eq(0).text()) + 1;
		var next = actual + 1;

		if (emptyWishlist) {
			wishlistCount.find('li').eq(0).text(actual);
			wishlistCount.find('li').eq(1).text(next);
		} else {
			wishlistCount.addClass('update-count');

			setTimeout(function () {
				wishlistCount.find('li').eq(0).text(actual);
			}, 150);

			setTimeout(function () {
				wishlistCount.removeClass('update-count');
			}, 200);

			setTimeout(function () {
				wishlistCount.find('li').eq(1).text(next);
			}, 230);
		}
	} else {
		var actual = Number(wishlistCount.find('li').eq(0).text()) + quantity;
		var next = actual + 1;

		wishlistCount.find('li').eq(0).text(actual);
		wishlistCount.find('li').eq(1).text(next);
	}
}

});
