<script type="text/javascript">
    var searchurl = "https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/ajax_search/";
    var frontpagesurl = "https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/frontpages/ajax";

    var input = document.getElementById('search');
    var keyword_suggest = new Awesomplete(input);

    var current_call = null;
    keyword_suggest.sort = false;

    $('#search').on('awesomplete-selectcomplete', function (e) {
        if (keyword_suggest.associative_array[$('#search').val()] != "") {
            window.location.href = keyword_suggest.associative_array[$('#search').val()];
        }
    });

    $(document).on('submit', '#quick_contact, #contact-form', function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();

        var form = $(this);
        var gdpr_checkbox = $(this).find('input[name=gdpr_privacy_policy]');

        if (!gdpr_checkbox.is(':checked')) {
            $.alert({
                title: "A aparut o eroare!",
                content: "Pentru a putea inregistra aceasta cerere te rugam sa accepti politica de confidentialitate a site-ului BebeTei!",
                columnClass: 'col-md-offset-3 col-md-6 col-sm-12'
            });
            return;
        }

        var name = $(this).find('input[name=name]').val();
        var email = $(this).find('input[name=email]').val();
        var comment = $(this).find('textarea[name=comment]').val();
        var recaptcha = $('#recaptchaResponse').val();

        $.ajax({
            url: frontpagesurl,
            data: {
                'action': 'quick_contact',

                'name': name,
                'email': email,
                'comment': comment,
                'g-recaptcha-response': recaptcha,

                'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
            },
            type: 'post',
            datatype: 'json',
            success: function (output) {
                if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
                    $.alert({
                        title: output.title,
                        content: output.content,
                        columnClass: 'col-md-offset-2 col-md-8'
                    });

                    form[0].reset();
                } else {
                    $.alert({
                        onClose: function () {
                            if (output.status_id == 1100) {
                                $(location).attr('href', 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/');
                            } else if (output.status_id == 1200) {
                                location.reload(true);
                            }
                        },
                        title: output.title,
                        content: output.content,
                        columnClass: 'col-md-offset-2 col-md-8'
                    });
                }

                grecaptcha.reset(quickContact_recaptchaID);
            },
            error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                $.alert({
                    content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                    title: "ERROR"
                });

                grecaptcha.reset(quickContact_recaptchaID);
            }
        });
    });

    $(document).on('submit', '#returns-form', function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();

        var form = $(this);

        var gdpr_checkbox = $(this).find('input[name=gdpr_privacy_policy]');

        if (!gdpr_checkbox.is(':checked')) {
            $.alert({
                title: "A aparut o eroare!",
                content: "Pentru a putea inregistra aceasta cerere te rugam sa accepti politica de confidentialitate a site-ului BebeTei!",
                columnClass: 'col-md-offset-3 col-md-6 col-sm-12'
            });
            return;
        }

        var option = $(this).find('option:selected','select[name=option]').val();
        var products = $(this).find('textarea[name=products]').val();
        var name = $(this).find('input[name=name]').val();
        var email = $(this).find('input[name=email]').val();
        var phone = $(this).find('input[name=phone]').val();
        var recaptcha = $('#recaptchaResponseReturnForm').val();

        $.ajax({
            url: frontpagesurl,
            data: {
                'action': 'formular_returnare',

                'option': option,
                'products': products,
                'name': name,
                'email': email,
                'phone': phone,
                'g-recaptcha-response': recaptcha,

                'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
            },
            type: 'post',
            datatype: 'json',
            success: function (output) {
                if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
                    $.alert({
                        title: output.title,
                        content: output.content,
                        columnClass: 'col-md-offset-2 col-md-8'
                    });

                    form[0].reset();
                } else {
                    $.alert({
                        onClose: function () {
                            location.reload(true);
                        },
                        title: output.title,
                        content: output.content,
                        columnClass: 'col-md-offset-2 col-md-8'
                    });
                }

                grecaptcha.reset(returnForm_recaptchaID);
            },
            error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                $.alert({
                    content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                    title: "ERROR"
                });

                grecaptcha.reset(returnForm_recaptchaID);
            }
        });
    });


    var typingTimer;
    var doneTypingInterval = 250;

    $(document).on('keyup', '#search', function (e) {
        if (e.keyCode == 13 || e.keyCode == 17 || e.keyCode == 27 || e.keyCode == 37 || e.keyCode == 38 || e.keyCode == 39 || e.keyCode == 40) {
            return;
        }

        clearTimeout(typingTimer);
        typingTimer = setTimeout(function () {
            doneTyping($('#search'));
        }, doneTypingInterval);
    });

    $(document).on('keydown', '#search', function (e) {
        clearTimeout(typingTimer);
    });

    function doneTyping(current_element) {
        if (current_call != null) {
            current_call.abort();
        }

        var current_search = current_element.val();

        if (current_search.length > 2) {
            current_call = $.ajax({
                url: searchurl,
                data: {
                    'action': 'search',

                    'q': current_search,

                    'ci_csrf_token': ci_csrf_token()
                },
                type: 'post',
                datatype: 'json',
                success: function (output) {
                    if (output.status_id >= 2000) {
                        var associative_array = {};
                        Object.keys(output.suggested_word_searches).forEach(function (key) {
                            associative_array[key] = output.suggested_word_searches[key];
                        });

                        Object.keys(output.possible_category_suggestions).forEach(function (key) {
                            associative_array[key] = output.possible_category_suggestions[key];
                        });

                        Object.keys(output.possible_products).forEach(function (key) {
                            associative_array[key] = output.possible_products[key];
                        });

                        var list = [];
                        Object.keys(associative_array).forEach(function (key) {
                            list.push(key);
                        });

                        if (current_element.attr('id') == 'search') {
                            keyword_suggest.associative_array = associative_array;
                            keyword_suggest.list = list;
                        }
                    }
                },
                error: function (jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                    // $.alert({
                    // 	content: "Unknown error has occured!",
                    // 	title: "ERROR",
                    // 	onClose: function() {
                    // 		location.reload(true);
                    // 	}
                    // });
                }
            });
        }
    }

    $(document).on('submit', '#searchForm, #search1', function (e) {
        window.location.href = "https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/search/" + $(this).find('input[name=q]').val();

        return false;
    });
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
$(document).on('click', '#closeCookie', function(e) {
	e.preventDefault();

	$('#cookiePolicy').hide();

	$.ajax({
		url: 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/frontpages/ajax',
		type: 'post',
		data: {
			'action':         'cookie_policy',

			'ci_csrf_token':   ci_csrf_token()
		},
		datatype: 'json'
	});
});

function shake() {
	var div = document.getElementById('gdpr_terms_and_conditions');
	var interval = 100;
	var distance = 10;
	var times = 4;

	$(div).css('position', 'relative');

	for (var iter = 0; iter < (times + 1) ; iter++) {
		$(div).animate({
			left: ((iter % 2 == 0 ? distance : distance * -1))
		}, interval);
	}
	$(div).animate({ left: 0 }, interval);
}

$('#tac_agreement').on('submit', function(e) {
	e.preventDefault();

	var newsletter_agr = $(this).find('input[name=newsletter]').is(':checked');
	var tac_agr = $(this).find('input[name=terms_and_conditions]').is(':checked');

	if (tac_agr) {
		$.ajax({
			url: 'https://comenzi.bebetei.ro/frontpages/ajax',
			data: {
				'action':          'privacy_policy',

				'tac_agr':         tac_agr,
				'newsletter_agr':  newsletter_agr,

				'ci_csrf_token':   ci_csrf_token()
			},
			type: 'post',
			datatype: 'json',
			success: function(output) {
				$('.terms_and_agreement_gdpr').fadeOut();
			}
		});
	} else {
		shake();
	}
});
